Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the quarks object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 209. The moduli space has dimension at most 189 with 2 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_209(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 209. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^189(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on quarks structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 451. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 409 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of structural decomposition and the invariant on the space spanned by quarks generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 451, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 409. QED.
